The national Worker is entitled to an end-of-service gratuity upon the end of his service,\nin accordance with the legislation regulating pensions and social security in force in the\nState.\n2. The foreign full-time Worker, who has completed one or more years of continuous service,\nis entitled to an end-of-service gratuity upon the end of his service, calculated according\nto the basic wage, as follows:\na. The wage of twenty-one (21) days for each year of the first five years of service.\nb. The wage of thirty (30) days for each year in excess of that.\n3. The foreign Worker is entitled to a bonus for parts of the year in proportion to the amount\nspent in work, provided that he has completed one year of continuous service.\n4. The days of absence from work without pay shall not be included in the calculation of the\nduration of service.\n5. Without prejudice to what is decided by legislation regarding the granting of pensions or\nretirement benefits to Workers in some Establishments, the end-of-service gratuity shall\nbe calculated according to the last basic wage received by the foreign Worker, for those\nwho receive their wages by month, week, or day, and according to the average daily wage\nstipulated in the provisions of this Decree by law for those who receive their wages by\npiece-meal.\n6. The foregoing is provided that the total end-of-service gratuity for the foreign Worker does\nnot exceed two years' wage."

The termination of the Worker's service by the Employer shall be unlawful if the\ntermination of the Worker's service is due to his filing a serious complaint to the Ministry\nor filing a case against the Employer that has been proven to be true.\n2. The Employer is obligated to pay a fair compensation to the Worker estimated by the\ncompetent court, if it is proven that the dismissal is unlawful in accordance with Clause\n(1) of this Article. The amount of compensation shall be determined taking into account\nthe kind of work, the amount of damage incurred by the Worker and the duration of his\nservice. In all cases, the amount of compensation must not exceed the wage of the Worker\nfor a period of three (3) months, calculated according to the last wage received thereby.\n3. The provisions of Clause (2) of this Article shall not prejudice the right of the Worker to\nthe warning allowance and the end-of-service gratuity entitled thereto in accordance with\nthe provisions of this Decree by law."

The amounts owed by the worker that are necessary for the repayment of loans or\namounts paid in excess to what he is entitled to.\nb. The repayment of amounts deducted for the calculation of contribution to pensions and\ninsurances, pursuant to the applicable legislation in the State.\nc. Amounts deducted from the worker because of violations committed subject to the list of\npenalties applicable at the establishment and approved by the Ministry.\nd. Debts owed pursuant to the execution of a court judgment against the worker.\ne. Amounts to repair the damages caused by the worker attributed to his mistake or violation\nof the employer’s instructions, which resulted in the damage, destruction or loss of tools,\nmachines, products, or substances owned by the employer.\n2. The employer shall perform the procedures stipulated in the Decree-Law and this resolution\nin the event the owed amounts resulted from violations the worker committed or as a result\nof damages attributed to his mistake, provided that a period of (3) three months has not\nlapsed on the amounts owed starting from the date they became due – unless otherwise is\nagreed."

      "text": "المادة (51)\nمكافأة نهاية الخدمة للعاملين بنمط الدوام الكامل\n1. يستحق العامل المواطن مكافأة نهاية الخدمة عند انتهاء خدمته وذلك وفقًا للتشريعات المنظمة للمعاشات\nوالتأمينات الاجتماعية السارية في الدولة.\n2. يستحق العامل الأجنبي الذي يعمل وفق نمط العمل بالدوام الكامل، والذي أكمل سنة أو أكثر في الخدمة\nالمستمرة مكافأة نهاية الخدمة عند انتهاء خدمته، تحسب وفق الأجر الأساسي، وذلك على النحو الآتي:\nأ. أجر (21) واحد وعشرين يوم عن كل سنة من سنوات الخدمة الخمس الأولى.\nب. أجر (30) ثلاثين يوم عن كل سنة مما زاد على ذلك.\n3. يستحق العامل الأجنبي مكافأة عن أجزاء السنة بنسبة ما قضاه منها في العمل بشرط أن يكون قد أكمل\nسنة من الخدمة المستمرة.\n4. لا تدخل أيام الانقطاع عن العمل بدون أجر في حساب مدة الخدمة.\n5. مع عدم الإخلال بما تقرره التشريعات بشأن منح معاشات أو مكافآت تقاعد للعاملين في بعض المنشآت،\nتُحسب مكافأة نهاية الخدمة وفق آخر أجر أساسي كان يستحقه العامل الأجنبي بالنسبة إلى من يتقاضون\nأجورهم بالشهر أو بالأسبوع أو باليوم ووفق متوسط الأجر اليومي المنصوص عليه في أحكام هذا المرسوم\nبقانون لمن يتقاضون أجورهم بالقطعة.\n6. يشترط فيما تقدم ألا تزيد مكافأة نهاية الخدمة للعامل الأجنبي في مجموعها عن أجر سنتين.\n7. لصاحب العمل أن يقتطع من مكافأة نهاية الخدمة، أي مبالغ تكون مستحقة قانونًا أو بحكم قضائي،\nوفق الشروط والإجراءات التي تحددها اللائحة التنفيذية لهذا المرسوم بقان ون.\n8. لمجلس الوزراء، بناءً على اقتراح الوزير وبعد التنسيق مع الجهات المعنية، إقرار أنظمة أخرى بديلة لنظام\nمكافأة نهاية الخدمة، ويحدد القرار الصادر منه، شروط وضوابط وآلية الاشتراك في هذه الأنظمة."

      "text": "المادة (15)\nمستحقات العامل حين وفاته\n1. على صاحب العمل، في حال وفاة العامل، تسليم أسرة العامل، أي أجور أو مستحقات مالية للعامل مضافًا\nإليها مكافأة نهاية الخدمة والتي يستحقها العامل وفق أحكام هذا المرسوم بقانون ولائحته التنفيذية، وذلك\nخلال مدة لا تزيد على (10) عشرة أيام من تاريخ الوفاة أو من تاريخ علم صاحب العمل بوفاة العامل.\n2. مع مراعاة حكم البند (1) من هذه المادة، للعامل أن يحدد خطيًّا الشخص المعني من أسرته باستلام حقوقه\nفي حال وفاته.\n3. يتحمل صاحب العمل كافة التكاليف المترتبة على تجهيز ونقل جثمان العامل المتوفى إلى موطنه الأصلي أو\nمحل إقامته إذا طلب ذووه ذلك.\n4. للوزارة وبالتنسيق مع الجهات المعنية وضع آلية للاحتفاظ بمستحقات العامل حال وفاته وتعذر تسليمها\nلأسرته أو أصحاب الحق فيها."

      "text": "Article (42)\nCases of Termination of the Employment Contract\nThe employment contract shall be terminated in any of the following cases:\n1. If the parties agree in writing to terminate it.\n2. Upon the expiry of the period specified in the contract unless it is extended or renewed in\naccordance with the provisions of this Decree by law.\n3. At the request of one of the parties, provided that the provisions of this Decree by law\nregarding the termination of the employment contract and the warning period agreed\nupon in the contract are abided by.\n4. The Establishment is closed permanently, in accordance with the legislation in force in the\nState.\n8.       "article": "47",
      "text": "Article (47)\nUnlawful Termination of Worker's Service\n1. The termination of the Worker's service by the Employer shall be unlawful if the\ntermination of the Worker's service is due to his filing a serious complaint to the Ministry\nor filing a case against the Employer that has been proven to be true.\n2. The Employer is obligated to pay a fair compensation to the Worker estimated by the\ncompetent court, if it is proven that the dismissal is unlawful in accordance with Clause\n(1) of this Article. The amount of compensation shall be determined taking into account\nthe kind of work, the amount of damage incurred by the Worker and the duration of his\nservice. In all cases, the amount of compensation must not exceed the wage of the Worker\nfor a period of three (3) months, calculated according to the last wage received thereby.\n3. The provisions of Clause (2) of this Article shall not prejudice the right of the Worker to\nthe warning allowance and the end-of-service gratuity entitled thereto in accordance with\nthe provisions of this Decree by law."

    {
      "id": "labour-law:en:51:1",
      "instrument": "labour-law",
      "article": "51",
      "text": "Article (51)\nEnd-of-Service Gratuity for Full-Time Workers\n1. The national Worker is entitled to an end-of-service gratuity upon the end of his service,\nin accordance with the legislation regulating pensions and social security in force in the\nState.\n2. The foreign full-time Worker, who has completed one or more years of continuous service,\nis entitled to an end-of-service gratuity upon the end of his service, calculated according\nto the basic wage, as follows:\na. The wage of twenty-one (21) days for each year of the first five years of service.\nb. The wage of thirty (30) days for each year in excess of that.\n3. The foreign Worker is entitled to a bonus for parts of the year in proportion to the amount\nspent in work, provided that he has completed one year of continuous service.\n4. The days of absence from work without pay shall not be included in the calculation of the\nduration of service.\n5. Without prejudice to what is decided by legislation regarding the granting of pensions or\nretirement benefits to Workers in some Establishments, the end-of-service gratuity shall\nbe calculated according to the last basic wage received by the foreign Worker, for those\nwho receive their wages by month, week, or day, and according to the average daily wage\nstipulated in the provisions of this Decree by law for those who receive their wages by\npiece-meal.\n6. The foregoing is provided that the total end-of-service gratuity for the foreign Worker does\nnot exceed two years' wage."
    },

      "instrument": "labour-law",
      "article": "53",
      "text": "Article (53)\nPayment of Worker's Entitlement at the End of the Contract\nThe Employer shall pay the Worker, within fourteen (14) days as of the date of the end of the\ncontract, his wages and all other entitlements stipulated in this Decree by law and the\nresolutions issued in implementation thereof, the contract or the Establishment's Articles of\nAssociation."
    },
    {
      "id": "labour-law:en:43:1",
      "instrument": "labour-law",
      "article": "43",
      "text": "Article (43)\nEmployment Contract Termination Warning\n1. Either party to the employment contract may terminate the contract for any legitimate\nreason, provided that the other party is notified in writing, and commits to work within\nthe warning period agreed upon in the contract, provided that the period is not less than\nthirty (30) days, and not more than ninety (90) days.\n2. The employment contract shall remain effective for the duration of the warning period\nreferred to in this Article, and end upon the expiry of the period, and the Worker shall be\nentitled to his full wage for that period according to the last wage received thereby, and\nhe must work during that period if the Employer asks him to do so. An agreement may be\nmade to exempt from the warning condition or reduce its duration while preserving all\nthe rights of the Worker for the warning period agreed upon in the employment contract.\nThe warning period must be the same for both parties unless it is in the interest of the\nWorker.\n3. The party who does not abide by the warning period must pay the other party a\ncompensation called a warning allowance, even if the failure to warn does not result in\nharm to the other party. Such compensation shall be equal to the Worker's wage for the\nentire warning period or the remaining part of it."
    },
    {
      "id": "executive-regulations:en:25:1",
      "instrument": "executive-regulations",
      "article": "25",
      "text": "Article (25)\nTermination of the Employment Contract due to the Employer’s Bankruptcy or\nInsolvency\nSubject to the provisions stipulated in Federal Decree-Law No. (9) of 2016 concerning\nBankruptcy, and Federal Decree-Law No. (19) of 2019 concerning Insolvency and the provisions\nof clause (8) of Article (42) of the Decree-Law:\n1. The employment contract shall be terminated in any of the following events:\na. Issuance of a court judgment declaring the employer’s bankruptcy or insolvency in\naccordance with the applicable legislation in the State in this regard.\nb. Issuance of a decision from the concerned authorities stating the employer’s inability to\nresume his activity for exceptional economic reasons beyond his control.\n2. The Ministry may, based on its sole discretion, cancel the worker’s employment permit\npursuant to the court judgment declaring the employer’s bankruptcy, and it may grant the\nworker a new permit in accordance with the controls approved in this regard."
    }

        "reasoning": "The law: Article 51(2) grants gratuity to foreign full-time worker upon end of service, regardless of who ends it. There's no distinction between resignation and employer termination in the current law. The old law (Federal Law 8 of 1980) had a reduction for resignation under 5 years, but the new law removed it. Article 68(3) mentions the employer may calculate gratuity per the old indefinite-term contract provisions — but that's transitional.\n\nThe user asks a general question about how the law calculates and whether it differs. I should answer: no difference under the current law; Article 51(2) applies upon end of service regardless of the reason. The gratuity is based on basic wage: 21 days' wage per year for first 5 years, 30 days per year beyond. For 4 years: 4 × 21 = 84 days of basic wage. Capped at 2 years' wage.\n\nShould I compute? They didn't give a wage. I can't compute without basic wage. I could offer. But the question is \"how does the law calculate\" — a general explanation.
